## More SVG Icons

## Copy & download Fluent Emoji Flat icons

Click any icon above to open the detail panel. Four export formats are available instantly, no sign-up required.

SVG

Raw vector markup. Paste directly into HTML or React components. Scales to any size without quality loss.

JSX / TSX

React-ready component. Drop into any React or Next.js project. Works with shadcn/ui, Radix, and Tailwind CSS.

PNG

Raster export at 1×, 2× and 3× for email templates, Figma imports, app assets, and Open Graph images.

Base64

Inline data URI, embed icons in CSS background-image, email HTML, or anywhere an image URL is needed.

Works with shadcn/ui

Copy the JSX format and paste directly into any shadcn/ui component. Alternatively, installand use via `@iconify/react`, fully tree-shakeable, no icon font loading required.

## How to use Fluent Emoji Flat icons

License SPDX

MIT

Grid Size

32px

Display Size

16px

Sample Icons

avocado, ticket, straight-ruler

Via Iconify · React

tsx

```
import { Icon } from '@iconify/react';

export default function App() {
  return <Icon icon="fluent-emoji-flat:icon-name" width={24} />;
}
```

Via Iconify · HTML

html

```
<script src="https://code.iconify.design/iconify-icon/2.1.0/iconify-icon.min.js"></script>
<iconify-icon icon="fluent-emoji-flat:icon-name" width="24"></iconify-icon>
```

## Frequently Asked Questions

Is Fluent Emoji Flat free for commercial use?

Yes. Fluent Emoji Flat is licensed under MIT, free for personal and commercial projects. [View license →](https://github.com/microsoft/fluentui-emoji/blob/main/LICENSE)

How do I download Fluent Emoji Flat SVG icons?

Click any icon in the browser above to open the detail panel. Choose raw SVG code to copy, download as SVG or PNG, export as a React JSX component, or get a Base64 data URI, all formats free, no sign-up required.

How many icons are in Fluent Emoji Flat?

Fluent Emoji Flat contains 3,145 SVG icons in the Emoji category, designed on a 32px grid.

Are Fluent Emoji Flat icons full-color or monochrome?

Fluent Emoji Flat icons are multi-color: they include built-in color information and do not need a fill or stroke applied. They render as-is without inheriting CSS currentColor.

What styles does Fluent Emoji Flat offer?

Fluent Emoji Flat includes icons in the following styles: Precise Shapes, Has Padding. You can filter by style in the browser above.

Is Fluent Emoji Flat organized into categories?

Yes. Fluent Emoji Flat is split into 9 categories including Activities, Animals & Nature, Flags, Food & Drink and 5 more. Use the category tabs in the browser above to filter.

Which icon collections include Fluent Emoji Flat?

Fluent Emoji Flat is included in 3 curated collections: Free SVG Emoji Icons: Twemoji, Noto, Fluent Emoji & More, Free Food & Drink SVG Icons, Free Hand Gesture SVG Icons.

Can I use Fluent Emoji Flat with React, Next.js or shadcn/ui?

Yes. Copy the JSX export from any icon's detail panel and paste it directly into your component. For ongoing use, install `@iconify-json/fluent-emoji-flat` and use it via `@iconify/react`, fully tree-shakeable, compatible with shadcn/ui, Tailwind CSS, Radix UI, and any React-based framework.
